## Founder action scripts

### Validate three different buying briefs

Start with metrobloks, Dataprana and AIB because their different published products can reject whole categories of land before spending.

[metrobloks property intake](https://www.metrobloks.com/realestate)

I source conditional land and utility-rights opportunities. Before sending a parcel, I want to confirm the exact market, first-phase MW, latest service date and transaction you would consider. Your published criteria suggest a particular search lane; is it active today?

- Which markets and first/ultimate MW are open now, and are those facility or critical IT MW?
- metrobloks: qualifying IXP/distance method, net acres, FAR and height? Dataprana: accepted ISO/utility territories and 10–50 MW ramp? AIB: actual 40–90 MW minimum and any smaller-phase exception?
- What customer commitment, power documents, fiber evidence and entitlement stage are required?
- Would you take an assignable contract, direct purchase, landowner JV or staged closing, and who pays for studies/collateral?

**Voicemail:** I am validating your current land-acquisition brief before submitting sites. Could the person responsible for site selection confirm active metros, phase size and evidence needed for an initial review?

**Success:** A named authorized reviewer, written brief, minimum submission package and next review date.

**Stop:** No active geography/product mandate or no acceptable route for a small originator.

### Test RadiusDC’s Denver expansion gap

The Thornton offering advertises dark fiber to RadiusDC’s fully leased 1500 Champa hub. This connects a site feature to a disclosed expansion search, although no parcel interest is verified.

[RadiusDC Denver expansion statement](https://www.radius-dc.com/insights-resources/denver-colorado)

Your Denver page says you are evaluating expansion. Would an existing Thornton facility with verified diverse dark-fiber rights to 1500 Champa and a roughly 7 MW facility service envelope fit that requirement? We would first validate actual vacant capacity, rights and conversion economics.

- Is the current need another carrier hub, a connected satellite, or larger purpose-built capacity?
- Does Thornton work for the actual customer latency and interconnection product?
- What minimum incremental IT capacity, coolant density, vacant-possession and service date are required?
- Will you review a broker-led acquisition packet before we pursue any control or paid studies?

**Voicemail:** I am checking a Champa-connected Thornton facility against your published Denver expansion need. Could your acquisitions or development lead confirm whether this location, scale and acquisition/retrofit structure are relevant?

**Success:** A named buyer reviewer accepts the specific product/location and states the minimum engineering and commercial evidence.

**Stop:** Wrong scale/product, existing pipeline already covers demand, unusable fiber rights, or tenant encumbrances prevent meaningful expansion.

### Reconcile Hinds before forecasting it

Current pages conflict on status and price; the exact 110-acre carve-out and service territory remain unresolved.

[Hinds listing / broker channel](https://www.land.com/property/110-acres-in-wilbarger-county-texas/27899900/)

Please confirm whether MLS 21317653 is available, the current price, exact county account and survey/legal description for the offered 110 acres. If it is available, would the owner authorize parcel-specific utility diligence and consider an assignable agreement with utility and buyer contingencies?

- Which current owner/entity has authority and what exact land is offered?
- What explains the active/sold and $319k/$352k/$385k differences?
- Which utility is authorized to respond for this tract, and can the existing AEP contact document the service path?
- What diligence, assignment, deposit and extension terms would the seller actually consider?

**Voicemail:** I need to reconcile the current Hinds offering before any further diligence: availability, exact tract, asking price and whether conditional control is possible. Please send the current offering package.

**Success:** One reconciled current offering and a written utility-intake path; then validate Dataprana/AIB phase fit.

**Stop:** Unavailable tract, unresolved owner authority or hard-money terms outside existing caps.

### One Newberry buyer/EDC sprint

Compare Dickert and I-26 Public Works only after DC BLOX or DartPoints identifies a real network/customer gap. Old per-acre marketing needs refresh.

[Dickert / Newberry property record](https://www.newberrycounty.gov/property/dickert-rail-site)

We are checking whether a small phased data-center opportunity serves a specific regional buyer need. Could you confirm current availability, exact parcels, owners, per-acre pricing and whether a roughly 20-acre initial tract with expansion rights is possible?

- Is there an unmet DC BLOX/DartPoints customer or network need here?
- Are Dickert and I-26 still offered, and what subdivisions/expansion rights are permitted?
- Who at the cooperative will provide a parcel/load/date-specific written response?
- Can two physically diverse carrier routes be built and at what order-of-magnitude cost?

**Voicemail:** I am comparing two Newberry industrial offerings against a buyer-specific phased-load requirement. Please send current parcel, pricing, utility and carrier information and the right development contact.

**Success:** A specific buyer reason to locate here plus refreshed owner/parcel and utility/carrier intake data.

**Stop:** Existing regional capacity already serves demand, or network/buildable-area constraints defeat the phase.

### Test Rocket’s advertised documents

Its advertised 50 MW path is a concrete document request. DataBank/CyrusOne should define the required Houston product before paid studies.

[CBRE Project Rocket offering](https://www.cbre.com/properties/properties-for-lease/datacenter/details/US-SMPL-210914/immediate-texas-data-center-opportunity-25-acres-cleared-and-platted-for-designated-50mw-use-houston-tx-00000)

Your offering references a CenterPoint-studied 50 MW path and expansion. Please provide the exact parcels, current availability, executed or draft utility documents, conditions, service dates and reliance/transfer rights. We are checking a named metro-capacity buyer fit before committing capital.

- What is executed versus studied, and what expires or requires collateral?
- Is the first 50 MW facility or IT capacity, and what is the actual deliverability date?
- Can a new buyer rely on/assume the studies, service agreements and land rights?
- What are the exact fiber routes, flood/drainage findings and current permit requirements?

**Voicemail:** I would like the documents behind Project Rocket’s advertised power path, exact offered acreage and current pricing/contingency terms for a buyer-led review.

**Success:** A reviewable utility/land package and a developer willing to compare it with owned Houston alternatives.

**Stop:** No transferable advantage, unclear offering perimeter or unaffordable hard control.

### Use Rural Hall to shape diligence with a buyer

Seller-funded study underway and a request for early buyer input make this a direct test of the buyer-first process.

[CBRE 8700 Circle Drive](https://www.cbre.com/properties/properties-for-lease/datacenter/details/US-SMPL-206024/8700-circle-drive-rural-hall-nc-27045)

The listing says the load study is funded and you welcome early buyer input. Could we align the study/site plan with a specific 40–90 MW developer brief, and clarify the rezoning process, exact parcels and control/assignment route?

- Which utility, applicant and phase are in the existing study?
- What is the rezoning docket, current status and realistic decision path?
- Can the eventual developer rely on or assume the study and service rights?
- What asking price and conditional structure would the owner discuss?

**Voicemail:** I am assessing whether a named developer’s phase requirements can inform the existing Rural Hall study. Please share study scope/timing, current entitlement docket and seller’s preferred transaction.

**Success:** AIB or another suitable buyer confirms this market/phase and is willing to guide the next study step.

**Stop:** Buyer needs a different market/product or study/land rights cannot be made usable.

### Qualify a conversion rather than raw land

TECfusions and Patmos have reuse precedents; the Mishawaka shell only helps if conversion beats new construction on cost and schedule.

[Former AM General plant offering](https://cressy.com/astro-property/?property_id=25447)

Please provide vacant-possession status, current equipment schedule, environmental reports, structural loading and transferable utility agreements. We are checking phased data-center conversion with a specialist buyer.

- What remains from the former electrical system and what is actually serviceable?
- Are former peak demand and prospective 50 MW claims supported by current utility documents?
- What contamination, roof/floor, cooling-yard, generator/noise and fire constraints exist?
- Will the owner consider buyer-backed exclusivity while a specialist screens conversion economics?

**Voicemail:** I am evaluating the former AM General property for a buyer-led technical conversion review. Please send the current sale/lease package, power-service documents and environmental/structural diligence.

**Success:** A specialist developer accepts the market and confirms a reusable technical/cost advantage.

**Stop:** Retrofit scope removes the time/cost benefit or environmental liabilities cannot be allocated.
